					<description><![CDATA[<p>Our new integrations let AI agents reason across Oracle’s business-critical data.</p>
<p>The post <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/microsoft-and-oracle-bring-agentic-ai-into-flow-of-enterprise-work/">Microsoft and Oracle bring agentic AI into flow of enterprise work</a> appeared first on <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog">Microsoft Azure Blog</a>.</p>
]]></description>
										<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>This blog is co-authored by Arpan Shah, SVP of Oracle Databases and Muneer Mirza, VP of Multicloud at Oracle Cloud Infrastructure (OCI).</em></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Today at Microsoft Ignite, we’re bringing agentic AI into the flow of enterprise work. Our new integrations let AI agents reason across Oracle’s business-critical data—from finance and supply chain to HR and customer operations—and the work context Microsoft Work IQ understands across meetings, email, documents, and Teams. Customers can access this combined intelligence through Excel, Teams, and Microsoft Copilot. Built on <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/solutions/oracle/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Oracle AI Database@Azure</a>, the integrations help businesses turn trusted enterprise data into insights and actions, with the choice, security, and performance mission-critical workloads demand.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/solutions/oracle/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Oracle on Azure</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">With Oracle AI Database@Azure, Oracle and Microsoft have helped customers modernize critical workloads to improve application performance and unlock more value from their enterprise data, by bringing databases closer to applications and connecting enterprise data with Microsoft AI. Oracle AI Database@Azure now spans 33 Azure regions, more than on any other hyperscaler, delivering the performance and flexibility customers need.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">At Oracle AI World 2026, we announced new capabilities for Oracle AI Database@Azure including FedRAMP Class D certification coming soon for US Government and public-sector workloads, and Proximity Placement Groups (PPGs) that enable customers to place their database closer to applications—resulting in approximately 50% lower VM-to-Exadata network round-trip latency than deployments without PPG alignment. Now, at Microsoft Ignite 2026, we’re building on that momentum.</p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="for-it-operations-monitoring-and-trust-at-every-layer">For IT operations: Monitoring and trust at every layer</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Today, we’re announcing limited preview of Azure Workload Center for Oracle, bringing each workload’s architecture, resources, and dependencies together through a construct called a Workload Virtual Instance (WVI). This significantly reduces the complexity and resources required to manage solutions at the individual component level. Integrations across the Workload Virtual Instance, Azure Monitor Health Models, and the <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Azure</a> Copilot Run Oracle agent connect monitoring signals across application, database, and infrastructure layers.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">This operational visibility is complemented by agent access controls: Oracle AI Database@Azure supports Microsoft Entra authentication and Oracle Deep Data Security, helping agents stay within users’ existing permissions while database calls run through the managed model context protocol (MCP) server and activity remains auditable across your Oracle database estate.</p>


<figure data-wp-context="{&quot;imageId&quot;:&quot;6afd09f38dacc&quot;}" data-wp-interactive="core/image" data-wp-key="6afd09f38dacc" class="wp-block-image aligncenter size-full wp-lightbox-container"><img decoding="async" data-wp-class--hide="state.isContentHidden" data-wp-class--show="state.isContentVisible" data-wp-init="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on--click="actions.showLightbox" data-wp-on--load="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on-window--resize="callbacks.setButtonStyles" src="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/azure_workload_health_upscaled.webp" alt="Azure Workload Center for Oracle showing degraded workload health for the Caldova Oracle workload. Copilot summarizes the issue and identifies database services as the cause, while the Run Oracle agent provides workload-aware troubleshooting and resolution options." class="wp-image-55525 webp-format" srcset="" data-orig-src="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/azure_workload_health_upscaled.webp"><button
			class="lightbox-trigger"
			type="button"

			</svg>
		</button></figure>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For example, after business users report poor performance from an order processing application, an admin must quickly diagnose and fix the problem. The admin signs in with their existing Microsoft Entra identity and uses Azure Workload Center for Oracle to get a unified view of workload health including signals across the infrastructure, database, and application layers while Copilot provides a summary highlighting where to focus.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The admin uses a natural-language prompt to initiate a coordinated investigation involving multiple agents, each with Oracle data access limited by their identify profile. Database calls from the agents run through the managed MCP server, and every action is captured for audit. Working together, the agents synthesize their findings into a single operational conclusion for the admin.</p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="for-end-users-oracle-data-in-the-flow-of-work">For end users: Oracle data in the flow of work</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Business users need insights from Oracle data, but they often need assistance from someone more technical to extract, format, and deliver it.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The new <strong><strong><a href="https://marketplace.microsoft.com/en-us/product/WA200008285?tab=Overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Oracle AI Database plug-in for Excel</a></strong></strong>, available today, lets users connect Excel directly to Oracle Database, pull trusted data into the worksheet, and reason over it with <a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/copilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Copilot</a> without leaving the spreadsheet. No export, no manual preparation, no ticket to IT.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Together, Microsoft and Oracle are bringing intelligence to the workflows that run the business: giving analysts, planners and controllers the power to understand margin changes, analyze shifts in demand, resolve reconciliation issues, and act on supply chain disruptions. Copilot analyzes Oracle transaction data and enriches it with context from <a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en/copilot/features/work-iq" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Work IQ</a>, drawn from meetings, email, documents and Teams, to identify exceptions, draft an explanation, and prepare a governed action plan for review. Rather than another dashboard, or disconnected chatbot, this is AI in the flow of work, grounded in systems of record maintained in Oracle AI Database.</p>


<figure data-wp-context="{&quot;imageId&quot;:&quot;6afd09f38eb66&quot;}" data-wp-interactive="core/image" data-wp-key="6afd09f38eb66" class="wp-block-image aligncenter size-full wp-lightbox-container"><img decoding="async" data-wp-class--hide="state.isContentHidden" data-wp-class--show="state.isContentVisible" data-wp-init="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on--click="actions.showLightbox" data-wp-on--load="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on-window--resize="callbacks.setButtonStyles" src="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/3_Excel_Addin_AI_Assistant_upscaled.webp" alt="Microsoft Excel connected to Oracle Autonomous A I Database, showing country sales data in a worksheet and an A I assistant retrieving and presenting results from the Oracle database." class="wp-image-55496 webp-format" srcset="" data-orig-src="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/3_Excel_Addin_AI_Assistant_upscaled.webp"><button

<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="for-developers-build-trusted-agents-quickly-from-prototype-to-production">For developers: Build trusted agents quickly, from prototype to production</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Pilots are easy; production is hard. Real processes span multiple data sources, call for several specialized agents, and demand answers a business can stand behind. Developers can build agents that connect to Oracle AI Database using <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/ai-foundry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Foundry</a> with Managed Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers and A2A protocol.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">MCP servers enable governed access from Azure agents to Oracle AI Database, whether it runs in Oracle Cloud Infrastructure or as Oracle AI Database@Azure, through approved SQL and PL/SQL operations, tools, and predefined reports. Application roles determine which tools each user or group can use, so agents never reach beyond what IT has sanctioned, and AI comes to the data rather than the data being copied out to AI. These integrations make Oracle AI Database a first-class source of knowledge, memory, and data for agents built in Microsoft Foundry.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>For example</strong>, a developer builds a sales intelligence application involving an agent retrieving account and order data from Oracle AI Database through MCP, a product agent identifying cross-sell opportunities, and a finance agent weighing in on account health. Through coordinated activity across these agents, the sales intelligence team receives a concise sales brief through an API. </p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">In another scenario, a customer service agent, grounded through <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/ai-foundry/iq/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Foundry IQ</a>, can answer questions about a policy or an account with supporting citations, and recall the customer history from Oracle-backed memory, retrieve similar past interactions, and responds with context.</p>


<figure data-wp-context="{&quot;imageId&quot;:&quot;6afd09f38f93e&quot;}" data-wp-interactive="core/image" data-wp-key="6afd09f38f93e" class="wp-block-image aligncenter size-full wp-lightbox-container"><img decoding="async" data-wp-class--hide="state.isContentHidden" data-wp-class--show="state.isContentVisible" data-wp-init="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on--click="actions.showLightbox" data-wp-on--load="callbacks.setButtonStyles" data-wp-on-window--resize="callbacks.setButtonStyles" src="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/foundry1_highlight_upscaled.webp" alt="Microsoft Foundry showing an A I agent connected to Oracle A I Database. 		<title>Introducing Azure Catalyst agentic modernization and new AI infrastructure innovations</title>
		<link>https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/introducing-azure-catalyst-agentic-modernization-and-new-ai-infrastructure-innovations/</link>

		<dc:creator><![CDATA[Jeremy Winter]]></dc:creator>
		<pubDate>Tue, 17 Nov 2026 18:35:00 +0000</pubDate>
				<category><![CDATA[AI + machine learning]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Compute]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Containers]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[DevOps]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Hybrid + multicloud]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Microsoft Ignite]]></category>
		<guid isPermaLink="false">https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/?p=55161</guid>

					<description><![CDATA[<p>At Microsoft Ignite 2026, we’ll showcase what’s possible when models, silicon, and cloud infrastructure converge into a single, co-engineered system. By tuning the entire stack, and introducing new capabilities across isolated execution, networking, context management, and inference efficiency, we’re fundamentally improving agent speed, security, and unit economics.</p>
<p>The post <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/introducing-azure-catalyst-agentic-modernization-and-new-ai-infrastructure-innovations/">Introducing Azure Catalyst agentic modernization and new AI infrastructure innovations</a> appeared first on <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog">Microsoft Azure Blog</a>.</p>
]]></description>
										<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">As AI continues to reshape how every business builds, operates, and competes, it is rapidly emerging as the defining workload in the cloud. This new class of agents, more demanding, complex, and dynamic than anything before them, is rewriting the rules of cloud infrastructure. Reasoning across multi-agent systems, drawing on rich context, acting in real time, and running continuously, they ask more of every layer of the platform beneath them. This week, we’re introducing innovations across <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/explore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Azure</a> that set a new standard for how the cloud powers AI: expanded AI core capabilities in Azure to build what’s next, and Azure Catalyst, an intelligent agent-led solution to modernize the apps, data, and infrastructure that already run the business.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For years, we have been reengineering every layer of Azure for the unique demands of AI and proving it on the world’s most advanced AI workloads, from leading frontier models to our own GitHub Copilot, Microsoft Teams, and Microsoft 365. That work has forged Azure into a single, co-engineered system, with models, silicon, and cloud infrastructure tuned as one and value compounding at every layer. This week, we take that even further.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/explore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Explore Microsoft Azure</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">New capabilities across isolated execution, agent networking, context management, and inference efficiency deliver faster, more secure agents with better unit economics. Every innovation rests on an open foundation, giving customers the freedom to run open models and open systems everywhere they operate. There is a wave of new AI applications scaling in production, and years of existing apps and data are moving to the cloud, becoming AI-ready. Azure was built for this moment.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Of everything we’re announcing this week, three areas matter most:</p>



<ol class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#ai-applications" id="#ai-applications">Building the next generation of AI applications</a>.</strong> Silicon, compute, storage, and networking work as one system across Azure for inference, training, and agents. A new connected path from Microsoft Foundry to Azure Kubernetes Service (AKS) gives you choice in how and where you run AI.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#azure-catalyst">Modernizing apps and data with agents</a>.</strong> Azure Catalyst uses AI agents to modernize applications, data<em>,</em> and infrastructure in a fraction of the time and cost of traditional methods, turning decades of technical debt into the fastest path to AI.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#run-ai" id="#run-ai">Extending AI wherever you operate</a>.</strong> Azure Local, flex nodes for AKS and Microsoft Foundry extend AI across cloud and distributed environments, governed through Azure Arc. Windows Server 2028, now in public preview, and Windows PCs, bring AI closer to applications, data<em>,</em> and users, providing the choice to meet workload-specific data, performance, regulatory<em>,</em> and cost needs.</li>
</ol>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="ai-applications">Build and run the next generation of AI applications</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/ai-foundry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Foundry</a> and <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/kubernetes-service/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">AKS</a> are at the center of how you build and run AI from Foundry’s managed experience, to the deep customization of AKS across models, runtimes, performance, security, and infrastructure.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The connected path between them runs from building AI applications and agents to running them at scale. Open-weight models and agents move seamlessly from Foundry to AKS clusters, deployed from cloud to edge, with consistent infrastructure, security policies, and governance.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/kubernetes-service/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Azure Kubernetes Service (AKS)</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Underneath these experiences, AI applications are challenging infrastructure in new ways: writing and executing code autonomously, connecting dynamically to multiple agents, models, tools, and data, and carrying context across long-running tasks. We&#8217;re advancing the infrastructure to meet each of these demands:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong>Isolation and confidentiality.</strong> With the Agent Sandbox extension for AKS, agent-generated code runs in secure, managed sandboxes with virtual machine (VM)-level isolation for every session, pre-warmed pools for fast startup, and a serverless option with no infrastructure to manage. Azure confidential computing extends protection to sensitive data and workloads while in use.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong>Agent networking and security.</strong> With Agent Grid, agents securely connect to other agents, models, tools, data, and Azure services through authenticated, encrypted connections. Identity-driven policies and default-deny network isolation control how agents communicate and what resources they can access.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong>Context and state.</strong> Long-context, long-running agents reuse processed prompt context instead of recomputing it on the GPU, so long tasks run faster and cost less. That cached context stays durable and controlled, by you, in your Azure tenant.</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">And we’re making Azure itself more intelligent, building in new workload-aware capabilities that combine deep context for what each AI application requires with what only the platform delivers: GPU and VM performance, topology, and real-time availability across the fleet. AKS AI Manager translates what each model needs into the right infrastructure, placing each deployment where the fleet has the GPU capacity to serve it best. The result is a cloud that makes every AI workload perform better, with faster responses, better economics, and optimal placement decisions.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">And across all of this, we’re extending our commitment to open-source, giving you transparency and access to the best innovation shaped by the community. Kubernetes, vLLM, and Ray, together with flex nodes for AKS in preview, bring greater flexibility from development through production, including supported GPU environments beyond Azure. You have the freedom to build on the best model, use the right framework, and run where your business needs it.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We’re already seeing what’s possible with these innovations. <strong>Rockwell Automation</strong> is using AI in its factories to help engineers diagnose equipment issues and investigate product failures. As these applications move into production, Rockwell wants to build on what works and bring those approaches to other facilities:</p>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Running our factory-level AI applications on Azure gives us one foundation for different AI workloads in production. When something works in one plant, we can adapt it for the next instead of starting over.</p>
<cite>—<strong>Justin Litherland, Vice President, Enterprise Program Management, IT, Rockwell Automation</strong></cite></blockquote>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Within our own teams, <a href="https://techcommunity.microsoft.com/blog/appsonazureblog/how-microsoft-365-built-a-platform-engineering-layer-on-aks-to-ship-faster-at-gl/4537523" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Microsoft 365 engineering built COSMIC</a>, a shared platform on AKS, to help them ship faster with built-in security, compliance, and reliability. Making infrastructure easier to run matters for developers. COSMIC handles infrastructure centrally so developers can focus on customer experiences rather than Kubernetes operations. What we learn as our own customer continues to shape future Azure capabilities for you.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">To go deeper at Microsoft Ignite, check out these sessions:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856637165001EL2e-1791588324684001CZEV?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK420</strong></a><strong>:</strong> Deploy and run Microsoft Foundry models and agents on Kubernetes</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856641427001EIEu-1791588175102001CcC9?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK448</strong></a><strong>:</strong> Run secure agent loops in production with isolated sandboxes at scale</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856638536001E9Fm-1791588157716001C6AV?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK429</strong></a><strong>:</strong> The evolution of Azure infrastructure for AI models, apps, and agents</li>
</ul>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/ai-foundry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Microsoft Foundry</a></div>
</div>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="azure-catalyst">Modernize applications and data with Azure Catalyst</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The need to modernize is universal. But with developers stretched thin, custom code and deep running dependencies, progress often stalls and the backlog keeps growing. Decades of technical debt adds security exposure, cost and complexity, while disconnected tools create friction from assessment to execution.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Agents in Azure Catalyst change that, analyzing legacy code, mapping dependencies, and planning and executing with precision. Work that once took months or years can now be done in weeks or even days, even with limited resources and skills, making it possible to modernize systems that were previously out of reach.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://aka.ms/Azure-Catalyst" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer noreferrer noopener">Learn more about Azure Catalyst</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The results are real: 1.4 million applications modernized, with businesses like <strong>Levi Strauss</strong> modernizing its enterprise systems and deploying more than 1,000 agents across them.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Azure Catalyst modernizes end to end—across applications, data, and infrastructure in one solution—delivered in the tools hundreds of millions of developers and IT professionals already use. Here’s what sets it apart:</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="one-connected-experience"><strong>One connected experience</strong></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Azure Catalyst connects IT teams and developers across an end-to-end process from discovery to design and execution. New capabilities add rich estate insights, AI-powered application discovery, modernization strategy and cost comparisons, and AI-generated migration waves. Execution spans applications, containers, storage, networking, and databases, and builds on existing coverage for Java and .NET modernization and migration support for VMware, Hyper-V, and physical servers.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="tailored-to-your-business"><strong>Tailored to your business</strong></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Governance rulebooks and bring-your-own-model support let you set the standards, guardrails, and models agents follow, so plans fit your architecture, priorities, and security requirements. Reusable runbooks and skills make what works repeatable, so every project meets your exact standards.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="secure-from-the-start"><strong>Secure from the start</strong></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Outdated applications and data increase security exposure. Azure Catalyst works with Microsoft Security to find, prioritize, and eliminate vulnerabilities end to end. Together they scan and rank risk, prioritize actions by severity and exposure, and flag vulnerable code and databases for modernization. Through <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/github/Copilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">GitHub Copilot</a> you can quickly remediate vulnerabilities as applications are modernized, fixing them once and shipping as both modern and hardened.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="ready-for-ai"><strong>Ready for AI</strong></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Azure Catalyst works in GitHub Copilot to help teams understand the business logic and dependencies of complex applications and upgrade those apps to run on Azure’s managed services. These upgrades make it easier to connect apps to AI workflows. New previews support modernizing applications and databases together and migrating from BizTalk to Azure Logic Apps. And because Azure Catalyst is built for Azure, it optimizes performance, security, and economics along the way.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-bloginabox-theme-ump-embed wp-block-msx-ump-embed" class="wp-block-bloginabox-theme-ump-embed" data-bi-mto="true" role="presentation">
	<div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/explore/customer-zero" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft is customer zero</a> for agent-led modernization, putting the same agentic tooling in Azure Catalyst across our estate. Microsoft Quantum and Xbox achieved 80% faster app modernization, while Intune upgraded a key library 75% faster than anticipated. GitHub ported the GitHub Copilot agentic harness—800,000 lines of production code—from TypeScript to Rust. By using these technologies ourselves, we learn firsthand what it takes to modernize complex, production-scale systems, from targeted fixes to large-scale code transformation, and apply those lessons to help you modernize your own environment.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For a full breakdown, read the <a href="https://aka.ms/modernization-ignite26" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Catalyst announcement blog</a>. To go deeper at Microsoft Ignite, check out these sessions:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856635948001E9jR-1791588116977001CUXt?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK412</strong></a><strong>:</strong> Rethink modernization end to end with agents</li>

<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856635643001EhaR-1791588196468001Ckj0?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK410</strong></a><strong>:</strong> Accelerating Java, .NET, and database modernization with agents</li>
</ul>

</div>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="run-ai">Run AI across cloud, edge, and devices</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Across cloud, enterprise infrastructure, edge, physical systems, and personal devices, we’re giving you the power to run AI where your business needs it. <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/local/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Local</a> and flex nodes for AKS extend cloud infrastructure and Kubernetes compute into distributed environments, while extending Microsoft Foundry across these environments. Azure Arc provides consistent infrastructure management and governance end to end. New at Microsoft Ignite, Windows Server 2028 enables AI inference alongside business-critical applications and data, while Windows PCs bring intelligence directly to the devices where people work through local processing. Together, you compose the right infrastructure for each AI workload to best meet data, performance, regulatory, and cost requirements. </p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For a full breakdown, read the <a href="https://aka.ms/WindowsServer2028Announcement" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Windows Server 2028 blog</a>. To go deeper at Microsoft Ignite, check out these sessions:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856636851001Evwu-1791588269795001CgI7?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK418</strong></a><strong>:</strong> What’s new and what’s next for Windows Server</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions?search=brk442&amp;sortBy=relevance" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>BRK442:</strong></a> Build sovereign AI with Azure Local and Foundry Local</li>
</ul>

</div>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="innovate-on-an-open-foundation-for-what-comes-next">Innovate on an open foundation for what comes next</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The architecture decisions you make today will carry your business for the next decade. Our job is to ensure the systems you depend on now can evolve into an AI-powered future, and that everything you build next runs on an open, co-engineered platform with choice over your models and where your workloads run. The real test of everything we’re introducing is how these solutions perform in your environment. We’ll keep listening and building alongside you.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">This week, I’m looking forward to Mark Russinovich, Chief Technology Officer (CTO), Deputy Chief Information Security Officer (dCISO), and Technical Fellow, Microsoft Azure and Pavan Davuluri, Executive Vice President, Windows and Devices, discussing why Azure and Windows are the foundation for mission-critical AI, from datacenters to the edge to a billion devices, at <a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions?search=INV643&amp;sortBy=relevance" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>INV643</strong></a><strong>:</strong> AI infrastructure from cloud to edge. We’d love to see you there.</p>

										<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<details class="wp-block-details has-h-4-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-details-is-layout-flow" open><summary>Azure at Microsoft Ignite 2026: The headlines</summary>
<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#ai-core" id="#ai-core">Azure AI core</a></strong>: AI on infrastructure you control.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#azure-cobalt-200" id="#azure-cobalt-200">Azure Cobalt 200</a></strong>: Our highest-performing general-purpose virtual machines (VMs).</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#azure-catalyst" id="#azure-catalyst">Azure Catalyst</a></strong>: Agent-led modernization from plan to code.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#agentic-operations" id="#agentic-operations">Agentic cloud operations</a></strong>: Agents that keep your cloud running well.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="#azure-horizondb-and-fabric-horizondb" id="#azure-horizondb-and-fabric-horizondb"><strong>Azure HorizonDB </strong>and <strong>Fabric HorizonDB</strong></a>: PostgreSQL for AI apps and analytics.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><a href="#fabric-apps" id="#fabric-apps">Fabric apps</a>: Governed apps built on trusted data.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item"><strong><a href="#microsoft-iq-platform" id="#microsoft-iq-platform">Microsoft IQ</a></strong>: Context for every agent.</li>
</ul>
</details>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Over the past year, my conversations with customers have changed. A year ago, the question was which model to choose. Now it&#8217;s how to put agents to work on the processes that run the business, and how to trust them to do it.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">With the same frontier models available to everyone, the advantage comes from what no one else has: your context. It&#8217;s how your business runs, what your people know, and the systems and data you&#8217;ve spent years building. That&#8217;s your IQ. It&#8217;s your intellectual property, so it should stay yours, and it should work wherever your people and agents do. Our job is to make every layer beneath your agents better, from the infrastructure that runs them and the estate they act on to the apps they power and the business context that tells them how your company works.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Azure is the foundation of Frontier Transformation. We engineer Azure as one system, so the knowledge an agent needs stays governed and the security you use to protect your people extends to the agents acting for them. And because we&#8217;re evolving the open, cloud-native platform you already rely on, you don&#8217;t have to start over.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://azure.microsoft.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">See what Azure can do for your business</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">As Satya Nadella shared at Microsoft Build in June, we added more datacenter capacity in 18 months than in Azure&#8217;s first decade. Combined with more regions than any other cloud provider, you can scale your AI workloads globally with confidence as demand grows.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">This week at Microsoft Ignite, we&#8217;re sharing what&#8217;s new across every layer of the platform. New capabilities spanning compute, storage, and networking let you run AI at scale with the control of your own infrastructure, without having to build, secure, and operate all of it yourself. Azure Catalyst brings agent-led modernization together in one experience from estate to code.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">PostgreSQL now has two homes at Microsoft: Azure HorizonDB, generally available for mission-critical scale, and the new Fabric HorizonDB, which connects your PostgreSQL apps to analytics and AI. And Fabric apps, now generally available, let your teams turn trusted enterprise data into working apps.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We&#8217;re also introducing Microsoft IQ, the enterprise context management platform that helps you build your own IQ and put it to work in every app and agent. It also gives your teams a path on Azure to build, run, and continuously improve those agents.</p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="run-ai-at-scale-with-the-control-you-choose">Run AI at scale with the control you choose</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">It starts with the infrastructure that runs your agents. As they take on real work, you should be able to run them on your terms, with your choice of models, your data, and where they run. That control lets you find the right balance of performance and cost for each workload and adapt as models and needs change.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">But control comes with real work and risk. Platform teams end up assembling and operating much of the AI stack themselves and stretching GPUs to keep up with shifting demand. Agents that reach models, tools, and data and run code on their own open new security gaps, putting sensitive data at risk.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Teams want the control of running their own AI infrastructure without taking on all the manual toil of building, securing, and operating it.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="ai-core">Building the AI core on Azure</h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">That&#8217;s what we&#8217;re bringing to Azure. New capabilities across compute, storage, and networking come together as one AI foundation, so you keep control of your models and workloads while Azure takes on the work underneath. Build AI with <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/ai-foundry/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Microsoft Foundry</a> in a fully managed experience and run it at scale with <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/kubernetes-service/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Azure Kubernetes Service</a> (AKS) when you need more control over performance, security, and cost.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			Ignite session: AI infrastructure from cloud to Edge		</h2>
		<p class="kicker__content">
							<a
					href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/en-US/sessions/1787856658187001Etji-1789751959279001dn4k?source=sessions"
					class="kicker__link"
					target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"				>
						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Azure sets up and tunes the infrastructure for you, so your teams spend less time on configuration and more time moving models and agents into production. Workloads are placed based on available capacity, so your GPU investment goes further as demand grows. Security is enforced centrally across agents, models, and tools, giving security teams consistent controls wherever agents run. And because it&#8217;s built on open-source projects, your workloads stay portable, including to your own servers outside Azure, keeping your options open as your needs change.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The result is AI that scales with your business, on infrastructure you control.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">To hear how organizations are operating AI at scale, join Mark Russinovich and Pavan Davuluri in the innovation session &#8220;<a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/sessions/1787856658187001Etji-1789751959279001dn4k?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">AI Infrastructure from cloud to edge</a>.” </p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="azure-cobalt-200">Strengthening every layer of your AI estate with Azure Cobalt 200</h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Agents reason, make decisions, and run continuously, and that&#8217;s reshaping what cloud infrastructure needs to deliver. That&#8217;s why we design Azure as one system, from silicon to servers to services. We pair our own Maia and Cobalt chips with the latest from NVIDIA and AMD, so every workload runs on the right silicon for the job. Our strategy focuses on building performance, efficiency, and security into every layer. The result is AI that&#8217;s faster and more economical to run at scale, on a roadmap you can plan around.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			Ignite session: cobalt 200&#8230;		</h2>
		<p class="kicker__content">

					class="kicker__link"
					target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"				>
						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a href="https://aka.ms/Cobalt200VMs-GA" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Cobalt 200 virtual machines</a>, our highest-performing general-purpose VMs, are now generally available. Designed from the ground up for scale-out, cloud native, and agentic AI workloads, these VMs deliver up to 50% better CPU performance compared to our Cobalt 100 VMs. Together with Maia, they give you an infrastructure you can plan around.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://aka.ms/Cobalt200VMs-GA" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Read about the latest Azure Cobalt updates in this blog</a></div>
</div>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="agent-led-modernization-and-operations-from-estate-to-code">Agent-led modernization and operations, from estate to code</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph" id="agent-led-modernization-and-operations-from-estate-to-code">Agents are only as useful as the systems they can act on. Much of what makes your company unique is built into your applications and databases and years of business logic your teams rely on. Modernizing puts existing apps and data to work in new AI-powered experiences, helps teams ship faster, and lowers the cost and risk of running older systems, creating a more adaptable foundation for growth.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Getting there isn&#8217;t easy. Modernizing can mean mapping complex systems and changing code that few people fully understand. Keeping the estate running well means sorting through constant signals with little knowledge on what matters most. And as agents take on more of that work, teams need to let them act while keeping the oversight your business requires.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">That&#8217;s why we&#8217;re bringing agents to both, from estate to code. Azure Catalyst helps you modernize applications, code, and databases from plan to execution. And new capabilities in Azure Copilot, Azure SRE Agent, and Azure Monitor help keep your cloud and databases running well, with people in control.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="azure-catalyst">Modernize for AI with Azure Catalyst</h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph" id="agent-led-modernization-and-operations-from-estate-to-code"><a href="https://aka.ms/azure-catalyst" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Catalyst</a> is a new unified experience for agentic modernization. It brings discovery, planning, migration, modernization, and code intelligence together, so IT teams and developers work from the same plan across infrastructure, applications, and data. Azure Catalyst modernizes the technology you already have into a foundation for AI, putting the business logic in your existing applications to work in new AI-powered experiences and agentic business processes.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			Ignite session: rethink modernization&#8230;		</h2>
		<p class="kicker__content">
							<a
					href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/sessions/1787856635948001E9jR-1791588116977001CUXt?source=sessions"
					class="kicker__link"
					target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"				>
						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For IT teams, <a href="https://aka.ms/ai-infra-ignite26" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Catalyst agent</a> extends from migration planning into deeper modernization and execution. For developers, GitHub Copilot modernization carries that plan into code in the tools they already use, with agent governance rulebooks and bring-your-own-model support that customizes your modernization project to the specifications of each business. And Azure Catalyst delivers a deeper understanding of how enterprise applications work, so teams can assess the impact of changes, modernize faster, and find where legacy processes could be reimagined with AI.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">

			Ignite session: modernize faster with ai&#8230;		</h2>

						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Modernization can also close security gaps. End-of-support runtimes, unpatched dependencies, and aging code widen your threat surface. Azure Catalyst works with Microsoft Security to fix that: Microsoft Perception and MDASH rank vulnerabilities across your estate and code, Azure Catalyst identifies vulnerable code and databases to modernize first, and GitHub Copilot turns those findings into fixes in the same pass. Each app comes out more secure than it went in.</p>

<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="agentic-operations">Keep your cloud running well with agentic operations</h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">As applications scale across hybrid infrastructure, microservices, and AI workloads, leading organizations are moving to operating models where insight flows directly into action. That&#8217;s the idea behind <a href="https://aka.ms/AgenticOps" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">agentic operations</a>: AI agents, guided by your intent, continuously observe, reason, and help take action across the cloud lifecycle. Signals feed coordinated workflows that improve performance, cost, and reliability as systems run, with people kept in control.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">At Microsoft Ignite, we&#8217;re bringing that model to more of your estate. <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/products/copilot" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Azure Copilot</a>&#8216;s deployment agent, now generally available, helps you go from requirements described in natural language to deployed cloud workloads. You can even extend agentic operations to Oracle and SAP with Azure Workload Center, now in limited preview.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			Ignite session: put ai agents to work&#8230;		</h2>

					class="kicker__link"

					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/products/sre-agent/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Azure SRE Agent</a> brings agentic operations into secure production environments, with new enterprise controls in preview, while Microsoft Entra Agent ID and Agent 365 give central teams a consistent way to discover, govern, and audit agents throughout their lifecycle. We&#8217;re also giving agents richer data and understanding so they can detect issues earlier, diagnose problems faster, and take more effective action. <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/monitor/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Azure Monitor</a> health models, now generally available, give agents a real-time view of your application architecture and business priorities, so they can recommend fixes.</p>

<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a id="_msocom_1"></a></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="build-the-next-generation-of-ai-apps">Build the next generation of AI apps</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Then there are the apps your agents power, where business data becomes business value. The question for every organization is how quickly it can move from idea to an app the entire business can rely on. Today, the people building AI apps span the whole organization. Developers are creating mission-critical systems and new apps with agents, while teams across the business are describing the app they need in Copilot. Each needs something different from the platform, but every app needs to connect to business data and run under governance IT can trust.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We&#8217;re building one foundation for all of them, and it starts with PostgreSQL, the database of choice for a growing share of developers. With Azure HorizonDB and Fabric HorizonDB, you can run PostgreSQL wherever your apps need it, from mission-critical systems to analytics and AI. Fabric apps builds on that foundation, so teams can turn business data into working apps, whether they start in code or in conversation.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="azure-horizondb-and-fabric-horizondb">Power your AI apps with PostgreSQL on Azure HorizonDB and Fabric HorizonDB</h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The apps developers build now reason over data, call models, and act for users, so the database underneath has to bring transactions, vectors, and AI together at mission-critical scale. <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/horizondb" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Azure HorizonDB</a>, now generally available, is a fully managed, PostgreSQL-compatible database built for that work. It delivers millisecond latency with built-in vector indexing, so your apps can find, reason over, and act on data in one place. Enterprise-grade security comes built in, and developers can work in tools they already know, including GitHub Copilot and Visual Studio Code.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			ignite session: data foundations for modern apps&#8230;		</h2>
		<p class="kicker__content">
							<a

					class="kicker__link"
					target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"				>
						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We&#8217;re also extending the power of PostgreSQL directly into Microsoft Fabric. <a href="https://aka.ms/Fabric-HorizonDB" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Fabric HorizonDB</a>, now in public preview, brings a serverless, Fabric-native PostgreSQL experience alongside SQL database in Fabric and Cosmos DB in Fabric. By bringing operational databases, analytics, and AI together on a unified, governed platform, developers can build intelligent applications with less data movement and complexity. Fabric HorizonDB will also serve as the PostgreSQL backend for Fabric apps.</p>

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">To simplify database management across environments, <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/fabric/database/hub/overview" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Database Hub in Fabric</a> is now generally available. It provides a unified experience to discover, monitor, and manage databases across Azure, on-premises environments, and Fabric, with Fabric Copilot highlighting what needs attention.</p>

<h3 class="wp-block-heading" id="fabric-apps">Turn ideas into governed enterprise-ready apps with Fabric apps, now generally available<a id="_msocom_1"></a></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Great ideas for apps come from every corner of the business, and with Copilot, people can build one in minutes. But taking it from prototype to production on trusted data remains the biggest hurdle. <a href="https://www.microsoft.com/microsoft-fabric/features/rayfin" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Fabric apps</a>, now generally available, bring together the backend services applications need with direct access to trusted data in Fabric, built-in security, and enterprise governance. Developers and coding agents build in code with open-source tools, and Fabric provisions everything from databases to authentication, with no infrastructure to manage. This makes it easier to turn ideas into enterprise-ready applications that can scale across the organization.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We&#8217;re also extending Fabric’s role as the data foundation for AI-powered applications. With the new Microsoft Copilot integration, now in Frontier public preview, anyone can describe the app they need and bring it to life, with the option to use Fabric as a ready-to-use data backend providing databases, storage, and data APIs. We’re also introducing Copilot Credits as a new usage-based billing option for Microsoft Fabric, providing greater flexibility alongside existing capacity-based options.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Power BI creators can now build more than static reports. With a new agentic app creation experience in Power BI Desktop, now in preview, they can turn their existing semantic models into interactive apps that capture input, update data, and support operational workflows.</p>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="microsoft-iq-platform">Ground every agent in your business context with Microsoft IQ<a id="_msocom_1"></a></h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Most organizations already have the knowledge their agents need. It lives in the semantic models your analysts built in Power BI, the documents and procedures your teams rely on, the conversations happening across Microsoft 365, and the systems of record you run every day, like SAP and Oracle. The problem is that it&#8217;s scattered, so every new agent starts from zero and every team rebuilds the same context.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">That&#8217;s why we&#8217;re introducing <a href="https://www.microsoft.com/ai/microsoft-iq" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Microsoft IQ</a>, the enterprise context management platform for building your organization&#8217;s IQ. It brings together Work IQ, Fabric IQ, Foundry IQ, Web IQ, and more than 350 connectors, so the knowledge your agents need is captured once, governed in one place, and reused by every agent and app. </p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">At the heart of Microsoft IQ are new business plugins, now available as part of the <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-365/admin/manage/get-started-frontier?view=o365-worldwide" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Frontier program</a>. Business plugins turn what your teams know into trusted context, skills, and tools that anyone across the business can reuse. Take a supply chain disruption. A business plugin can tell an agent to use the components and orders semantic model in Fabric IQ to find the affected orders, the supplier contracts in Foundry IQ to check late-delivery terms, and Work IQ to find who owns the decision. Your domain experts write it once, and every agent across the organization inherits it.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Teams can also reuse proven evaluations across agents instead of rebuilding them. The foundation underneath Microsoft IQ brings Microsoft Foundry and Microsoft Fabric together in one path, built on Azure, to build, run, and continuously improve agents. And Agent 365 gives IT one place to observe, govern, and secure them.</p>



<div class="alignright wp-block-bloginabox-theme-kicker" data-bi-an="Kicker Right">
	<div class="kicker">
		<h2 class="kicker__title">
			ignite session: Harnessing Your IQ&#8230;		</h2>

						Watch now							↗</a>
					</p>
	</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Your IQ stays yours, and it isn&#8217;t locked into one assistant. The same context and business plugins reach your agents wherever they run, in Microsoft Copilot, GitHub Copilot, Microsoft Foundry, and assistants such as Claude and ChatGPT, through model context protocol (MCP) and APIs. Your permissions and governance go with it. Agents can also draw on the business context in your systems of record, like SAP and Oracle, so they work from the data your teams already rely on. Learn more about <a href="https://aka.ms/Oracle-MicrosoftIQ" id="https://aka.ms/Oracle-MicrosoftIQ" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Oracle’s latest integrations with Microsoft IQ</a>.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">See how the Australian Energy Market Operator uses Microsoft IQ to help keep Australia&#8217;s power grid secure:</p>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="experience-these-innovations-at-microsoft-ignite">Experience these innovations at Microsoft Ignite</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Every agent you build is only as good as what it knows about your business. That&#8217;s why we&#8217;re making sure your IQ, the context that makes your company unique, is available to your agents across every layer of Azure, with governance and security built in. It stays yours, and its value grows with everything you build on it. To learn more about Microsoft IQ, I encourage you to join my Microsoft Ignite session, “<a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/sessions/1787856657893001Euld-1789751958877001dRyn?source=sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Harnessing Your IQ with a continuously improving agent platform</a>,” in person or online at no cost, or watch the recording afterward.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For a closer look at all the innovation we are bringing to Azure, explore the full list of sessions on the <a href="https://ignite.microsoft.com/sessions" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Microsoft Ignite session catalog</a>.</p>

					<description><![CDATA[<p>Azure Cobalt 200 VMs bring improved performance and efficiency to cloud-native applications, databases, developer tools, and agentic AI workloads.</p>
<p>The post <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/azure-cobalt-200-vms-now-available-proven-on-real-workloads-and-built-for-the-agentic-era/">Azure Cobalt 200 VMs now available, proven on real workloads and built for the agentic era</a> appeared first on <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog">Microsoft Azure Blog</a>.</p>
]]></description>
										<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Today at Microsoft Ignite 2026, we&#8217;re announcing the general availability of <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/virtual-machines/sizes/cobalt-overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Azure Cobalt 200-based virtual machines (VMs)</a>. Cobalt 200 VMs deliver <strong><a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/blog/new-azure-cobalt-200-vms-deliver-50-performance-improvement-fully-optimized-for-modern-agentic-ai-workloads/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">up to 50% better performance than Cobalt 100</a></strong> and are now available in 14 regions, expanding to <strong>23 regions </strong>by the end of 2026. Built for cloud-native, data-intensive, and agentic AI workloads, Cobalt 200 VMs will represent <strong>a significant share of new Azure Compute capacity</strong> beginning in 2027.</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-content-justification-center is-layout-flex wp-container-core-buttons-is-layout-a89b3969 wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button"><a class="wp-block-button__link wp-element-button" href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/virtual-machines/sizes/cobalt-overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Explore Cobalt 200 VM sizes and deployment guidance</a></div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For customers like <strong>Amadeus, Databricks, InMobi, OpenAI, and Snowflake</strong>, Cobalt 200 VMs deliver improved performance and efficiency over Cobalt 100 VMs. As a result, customers can deliver faster search results, more responsive database queries, and run their services at higher utilization with fewer cores.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Cobalt 200&#8217;s performance and efficiency gains reflect our systems approach to infrastructure design, which co-optimizes silicon, software, networking, power, and rack infrastructure. Cobalt 200 VMs are also designed for agentic AI workflows, including agent sandboxes, file search, code compilation, and tool execution, where higher per-core performance can help reduce latency.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">As customer need for high-performance and low-latency compute increases, we’re adding new virtual machine types to bring the price-performance benefits of Cobalt to additional workloads: </p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li class="wp-block-list-item">Mpsv4 for high memory workloads.</li>



<li class="wp-block-list-item">Lpsv5 for workloads needing fast access to large datasets.</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">With <a href="https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/products/virtual-machines/boost" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Azure Boost</a> included in every Cobalt 200 VM, customers get the latest accelerated network and remote storage performance in <a href="http://azure.microsoft.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Microsoft Azure</a>.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-bloginabox-theme-ump-embed wp-block-msx-ump-embed" class="wp-block-bloginabox-theme-ump-embed" data-bi-mto="true" role="presentation">
	<div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">

<h2 class="wp-block-heading" id="proven-on-critical-customer-workloads">Proven on critical customer workloads</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Our benchmarking for Cobalt 200 VMs showed significant performance increases for a wide variety of workloads. We observed up to 1.5 times increased performance for web serving workloads, up to 1.6 times for Java-based workloads, up to 1.5 times for AI agent tool execution workloads, and 1.9 times for database workloads over Cobalt 100.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We’ve seen these results translate into real-world improvements as our customers bring <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/virtual-machines/sizes/cobalt-overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cobalt 200 VMs</a> to their own workloads.</p>



<details class="wp-block-details has-h-5-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-details-is-layout-flow" open><summary>See what customers are saying about Cobalt 200 VMs</summary>
<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="has-body-medium-font-size wp-block-paragraph">Cobalt has helped us sustain ChatGPT’s growth. Cobalt 100 gave us more CPU capacity when we needed it. By the time we tested Cobalt 200, our services already ran on ARM, so we could compare the same ChatGPT workloads on both generations. We saw higher throughput and lower latency on Cobalt 200. That means we can serve more users with the same number of cores, and they spend less time waiting for ChatGPT to start responding.</p>
<cite>—<strong>Vadim Filanovsky, Member of Technical Staff, OpenAI</strong></cite></blockquote>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="has-body-medium-font-size wp-block-paragraph">Genie, the data-smart AI coworker, puts answers in front of business users in plain language, and those answers depend on fast, efficient compute underneath. Azure Cobalt helps us deliver that. Azure Databricks serverless compute on Cobalt starts faster and runs more efficiently, so customers spend less time waiting and more time acting on their data. Cobalt 200 raises the bar again, and as we bring more of Azure Databricks onto it, Genie questions, SQL queries, and data pipelines get faster.</p>
<cite><strong>—Vinod Marur, SVP Engineering, Databricks</strong></cite></blockquote>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="has-body-medium-font-size wp-block-paragraph">Snowflake has grown with Arm on Azure, from the first Arm-based v5 VMs to Cobalt 100 and now Cobalt 200. Each generation has brought real performance and efficiency gains for our workloads, and those gains help us give our customers an even better experience. We’re proud to partner with Microsoft on what comes next for Cobalt and excited to see Cobalt 200 reach general availability.</p>
<cite><strong>—Murray Stokely, Distinguished Engineer, Snowflake</strong></cite></blockquote>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="has-body-medium-font-size wp-block-paragraph">Performance at scale is at the heart of how Amadeus serves the travel industry. In our benchmark lab, Azure Cobalt 200 improved flight search throughput by more than 30% compared with Cobalt 100, a performance gain that exceeded our expectations.</p>
<cite><strong>—Luc Choubert, VP Engineering, Amadeus</strong></cite></blockquote>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="has-body-medium-font-size wp-block-paragraph">We tested our ad-serving workloads on Cobalt 200 and saw clear performance and efficiency improvements. In addition to traditional workload patterns, we run high-throughput local neural network inference using SIMD, with each 32-core machine processing approximately 50,000 inferences per second. At the scale of a large fleet, these per-machine efficiency gains compound significantly, making Cobalt 200 particularly compelling for our workloads.</p>
<cite><strong>—Sudheer Bhat, Principal Engineer, InMobi</strong></cite></blockquote>
</details>



<details class="wp-block-details has-h-5-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-details-is-layout-flow" open><summary>Partner endorsements</summary>
<blockquote class="wp-block-quote has-quote-default-font-size is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
